Ablation studies show that ACERT's gains come from emotional and conversational continuity, rather than from speaker identity or acoustic conditions.

</details>


### [171] [How to Estimate Whether You Have Found Several Needles in a Haystack: Measuring Calibration in Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26468)
*Sophie Henning,Georg Hofmann,Alexander Schulte,Alexander Fraser,Annemarie Friedrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出多标签分类的置信度校准度量研究不足，现有分箱方案在大量负标签主导时会导致校准误差被低估、仅反映标签频率或产生稀疏分箱。作者提出一种对正负标签分配赋予相等权重的新分箱方案，用于计算标签级期望校准误差，并在层次多标签与极端多标签分类中验证其能给出更有意义的校准误差估计，同时揭示大语言模型多标签置信度校准仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置信度分数应被校准以匹配预测正确的真实概率，这是决定是否信任自动预测的关键。现有校准度量主要面向二分类或多分类任务，多标签校准研究严重不足；而多标签任务（如临床文本医学编码、新闻主题标注）通常由大量不适用标签构成的负标签主导，导致已有分箱方案计算标签级期望校准误差时存在低估误差、仅反映标签频率、或出现大量样本极少的箱等问题，因而需要更可信的标签级校准误差度量。

Method: 提出一种新的分箱方案（binning scheme），在计算标签级期望校准误差时为正标签分配与负标签分配赋予相等权重，以缓解负标签主导带来的偏差；并据此开展实证研究，在层次多标签分类与极端多标签分类任务中对比现有分箱方案所给出的校准误差估计。此外，还对大语言模型多标签预测的置信度校准进行研究分析。

Result: 实证研究表明，与现有分箱方案相比，新分箱方案在层次多标签分类和极端多标签分类中能够产生更有意义的校准误差估计；同时发现对大语言模型的多标签预测置信度进行校准仍是一个开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本文通过细致分析为多标签分类校准研究奠定基础，提供了一个用于衡量多标签校准的可靠评估指标；并指出校准大语言模型多标签预测置信度是未来有待解决的问题。

Abstract: A key factor in deciding whether to trust an automatic prediction is its confidence score, which should be calibrated to match the actual probability of the prediction being correct. Most confidence calibration metrics target binary or multi-class tasks, while multi-label calibration remains largely underexplored. Multi-label classification tasks, such as assigning medical codes to clinical notes or determining news topics, are usually dominated by a large number of negatives, i.e., labels that do not apply. We show that existing binning schemes to compute label-wise expected calibration error either underestimate the error, simply reflect label frequency, or suffer from many bins with very few instances. To achieve trustworthy label-wise calibration errors, we propose a new binning scheme that gives equal weight to positive and negative label assignments. Our empirical study demonstrates that in contrast to existing binning schemes, our new scheme results in meaningful estimates of calibration error in hierarchical and in extreme multi-label classification. We also show that calibrating confidence scores of large language models for multi-label predictions is an open challenge. Our detailed analysis lays the foundation for further research by providing a solid evaluation metric for measuring calibration in multi-label classification.

</details>


### [172] [Spoken Language Models that Think Aloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26488)
*Junyi Ao,Kainan Peng,Mingbo Ma,Shun Zhang,Zhenyu Tang,Xutai Ma,Xiang Li,Yinghao Li,Yuancheng Wang,Zhizheng Wu,Haizhou Li,Qing He,Xubo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出面向SLM的异步"边想边说"框架，在Thinker-Talker架构中并行运行推理流与轻量化的think-aloud流，缓解串行think-then-speak带来的长静默，保持回答准确率的同时显著降低可感知静默。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提升了语言模型能力，但直接用于口语语言模型（SLM）时，串行的"先想后说"范式会在推理阶段产生长时间静默，破坏实时口语交互的体验。

Method: 在Thinker-Talker架构内构建异步think-aloud框架：一个主推理流负责逻辑推演，一个轻量think-aloud流根据用户输入与演进中的推理状态生成简短、任务相关的进度话语；并通过动态平衡策略在运行时协调两流——在出现静默间隙时触发额外think-aloud语音，在最终回复就绪时取消待播话语。

Result: 在口语推理与问答基准上，该方法在保持与串行"先想后说"基线相当的回答准确率的同时，显著减少了用户可听到的推理过程静默。

Conclusion: 异步think-aloud为SLM提供了一种兼顾推理准确性与交互响应性的可行路径，展现了在实时口语交互中缓解静默问题的潜力。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has improved the capability of language models, directly applying it to Spoken Language Models (SLMs) may introduce long silent intervals under the serial "think-then-speak" paradigm, disrupting real-time spoken interaction. To address this issue, we propose an asynchronous think-aloud framework for reasoning-based SLMs within the Thinker-Talker architecture. The framework maintains a primary reasoning stream for logical deduction and a lightweight think-aloud stream that generates short, task-grounded progress utterances conditioned on the user input and the evolving reasoning state. A dynamic balance strategy coordinates the two streams at runtime, triggering additional think-aloud speech to avoid silent gaps and canceling pending utterances when the final response becomes ready. Experiments on spoken reasoning and question-answering benchmarks show that our approach substantially reduces user-audible silence during reasoning while maintaining answer accuracy comparable to that of a serial "think-then-speak" baseline, demonstrating the potential of asynchronous think-aloud for responsive interaction in SLMs.

</details>


### [173] [Calibration as a First-Class Criterion in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26489)
*Mario Sanz-Guerrero,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出语言模型校准虽已有成熟测量方法，但在NLP研究中普遍未被采用，形成"采纳缺口"；作者主张各子领域应将校准分数与主性能指标并列报告，把校准视为模型的必备属性，并指出现有基准多已具备所需输入，仅开放式生成仍是待解难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准（置信度与经验正确性的一致性）虽是NLP中研究充分的子领域，但在此子领域之外，研究者在引入新模型、数据集和基准时普遍不检查置信度分数是否有意义，这一"采纳缺口"成为可信LLM评估的主要障碍。原因在于：部署阶段过度自信的错误会带来实际危害，而研究流程内部的LLM-as-a-judge、合成数据生成、主动学习等方法都依赖校准的置信度却未加验证。

Method: 这是一篇立场/倡议性论文，非实验性方法。核心论证是：标准校准指标对每个样本仅需两个输入——置信度分数与正确性判断，而当前大多数基准已同时提供这两者，因此校准可以立即被报告。作者据此呼吁各NLP子领域将主要性能指标与校准分数配对报告，并建议将校准作为每个模型的必备属性而非小众话题；同时指出开放式生成场景下这两个输入的定义仍是开放挑战。

Result: 论文未提供新实验数据，主要结果为一组论断与建议：校准的采纳缺口是可信LLM评估的重要障碍；校准失准在部署与研究流程两类场景中均造成问题；技术上校准报告的即时性已具备（因基准已含所需两输入），主要瓶颈是采纳而非方法；开放式生成的校准度量仍待解决。

Conclusion: 作者结论是：NLP各子领域应为其主性能指标搭配校准分数，并将校准视为每个模型的基本属性而非小众话题；同时需进一步研究开放式生成中的校准定义，以推动校准在更广研究实践中的采纳，从而提升LLM评估的可信度。

Abstract: Calibration of language models -- the alignment between expressed or implicit confidence and empirical correctness -- is a well-studied subfield within NLP. Methods to measure it already exist. The problem is adoption: outside this subfield, NLP research regularly introduces new models, datasets, and benchmarks without checking whether the model's confidence scores are meaningful. We argue that this adoption gap is a major obstacle to trustworthy LLM evaluation. Miscalibration causes problems in two distinct areas: at deployment, where overconfident mistakes cause real harm, and inside the research pipeline, where methods like LLM-as-a-judge, synthetic data generation, and active learning rely on calibrated confidence without verifying it. Standard calibration metrics only require two inputs per example: a confidence score and a correctness judgment. Most benchmarks in use today already provide both, meaning calibration can be reported immediately. For open-ended generation, however, defining these two inputs is still an open challenge. We argue that each NLP subfield should pair its main performance metric with a calibration score and call for treating calibration as an essential property of every model rather than a niche topic.

</details>


### [174] [A Semiotics-Aware Framework for Evaluating Fidelity and Coverage in Natural Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26527)
*Lorenzo Zangari,Davide Picca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估文本间"符号对齐"的框架，通过符号画像（含语境意义与话语指涉）计算符号保真度与符号覆盖度两个指标，发现覆盖度通常低于保真度，且LLM与人工数据的对齐在低温采样时最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于词汇重叠或整篇相似度的标准指标，在评估描述同一表达的两段文本时，无法检测其在"表达框架方式"上的有意义差异。

Method: 构建符号对齐评估框架，将文本表示为符号画像（semiotic profile），涵盖语境意义与凸显的话语指涉；据此计算两个分数：符号保真度（一方画像被另一方支持的程度）与符号覆盖度（对另一方画像的复原程度）；并考察LLM在不同采样温度下与人工标注数据的对齐情况。

Result: 覆盖度通常低于保真度；LLM与人工整理数据的对齐在低采样温度下最高，温度升高则对齐度下降。

Conclusion: 符号对齐框架能捕捉传统词汇/整篇相似度指标遗漏的框架差异，保真度与覆盖度的不对称性及温度对LLM对齐的影响为文本表达评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: When two texts describe the same expression, standard metrics based on lexical overlap or whole-text similarity may fail to detect meaningful differences in how that expression is framed. We propose a framework to evaluate semiotic alignment between texts, where a semiotic profile encompasses both the contextual meaning and the discourse references made salient by a text. Our approach yields two scores, Semiotic Fidelity and Semiotic Coverage, estimating how much of one text's profile is supported by the other and how much of the other's profile it recovers. Experiments show that coverage is typically lower than fidelity, and that alignment between LLMs and human-curated data is highest at low sampling temperatures, while higher temperatures reduce this alignment.

</details>


### [175] [Transcribe, Translate, and Optimize: Joint Reward Learning for Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26536)
*Yanghe Dong,Wanting Huang,Weiran Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于GRPO的联合识别与翻译微调方法，缓解CoT语音翻译中SFT参考转写与推理生成转写不匹配的问题，在提升识别与翻译质量上均优于SFT和Direct ST。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT式语音翻译在SFT阶段使用参考转写，而推理阶段依赖模型自生成转写，二者存在训练-推理不匹配问题，限制了性能。

Method: 采用群体相对策略优化（GRPO）进行联合识别与翻译微调，对转写和译文分别打分，其中译文以模型生成的转写为条件，并比较三种token优势策略；使用Qwen2.5-Omni-3B，在四种语言上于CoVoST 2训练、CoVoST 2与FLEURS测试，对比SFT与GRPO下的CoT与Direct ST。

Result: CoT GRPO较Direct ST GRPO平均BLEU分别提升1.77（CoVoST 2）和0.83（FLEURS）；较CoT SFT，BLEU分别提升0.82和0.67，WER相对降低8.8%和7.2%。

Conclusion: 强化学习微调（GRPO）可有效缓解训练-推理不匹配，并同时提升语音识别与翻译性能。

Abstract: In LLM-based speech translation, transcription-based chain-of-thought (CoT) suffers from a mismatch between reference transcripts used in supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and model-generated transcripts at inference. To address this, we propose joint recognition and translation fine-tuning via group relative policy optimization (GRPO). We score both transcripts and translations, with translation conditioned on model-generated transcripts, and compare three token advantage strategies. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-3B across four languages, we evaluate CoT against direct speech translation (Direct ST) under SFT and GRPO, training on CoVoST 2 and testing on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. CoT GRPO outperforms Direct ST GRPO by 1.77 and 0.83 average BLEU points on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. Compared to CoT SFT, GRPO boosts BLEU by 0.82 and 0.67 points and reduces word error rate (WER) by 8.8% and 7.2% relatively. These results highlight reinforcement fine-tuning as an effective method to mitigate the training-inference mismatch, jointly improving recognition and translation.

</details>


### [176] [A retrospective analysis on the use of LLMs to study infant syntax learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26539)
*Hélie Bazin,Anouk Barberousse,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对BabyLM等利用大语言模型研究婴儿句法习得的研究进行了认识论评估，指出其方法论存在显著假设，且发展真实性语料对基准表现影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多用于探究儿童早期句法习得，BabyLM挑战是代表性研究计划。作者希望从认识论层面审视这些研究，评估其理论与方法基础的可靠性。

Method: 对BabyLM研究计划中的若干研究进行认识论评估，考察数据集构建、模型选择、训练方式及句法评估方法。

Result: 发现BabyLM及相关研究的方法论存在显著假设，削弱了其理论解释力；同时，使用发展真实性语料对模型在常用基准上的表现影响有限。

Conclusion: LLM与婴儿句法学习者之间可能存在重要计算差异，因此用LLM推断婴儿句法习得需谨慎。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly been used to investigate how children acquire syntax at an early stage of development. This is notably the central scientific goal of the BabyLM challenge, a community-wide effort to develop models that achieve human-level syntactic performance while being trained on developmentally realistic corpora. In this paper, we reflect on the use of LLMs in the study of infant syntax learning by providing an epistemological assessment of several studies from this research program. We discuss how datasets are built, which models are implemented, how they are trained and syntactically evaluated. We observe significant assumptions in the methodology of BabyLM and related studies, thus mitigating their theoretical scope. We additionally observe that using developmentally-realistic corpora have limited effects on models performance on commonly-used benchmarks, which suggest important computational differences between LLMs and the infant syntax learner.

</details>


### [177] [Receptiveness, Not Sycophancy: Distinguishing Engagement from Deference in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26579)
*Calvin Isley,Johann Gaebler,Max Lamparth,Julia Minson,Sharad Goel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出社会谄媚（sycophancy）评估存在构念效度问题：其识别的标记与对话接受性（conversational receptiveness）高度重叠，可能误罚有益行为；作者通过实验证明接受性可被偏好，并提出兼顾接受性与实质独立性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的谄媚倾向（尤其是社会谄媚中的肯定与积极表达）受到关注，但这些社会谄媚标记同时也是社会心理学中"对话接受性"的特征，而接受性被证明能改善分歧中的互动。这种重叠使社会谄媚评估面临构念效度问题：可能把有益行为误判为谄媚。

Method: 使用流行的道德建议数据集进行检验；对人工撰写的回复在保持实质结论不变的前提下提升接受性，观察其社会谄媚分类变化；采用预注册实验，让参与者比较实质等价的回复，评估偏好、倾听意愿与求助意愿（包括认为提问者有错的参与者）；提出一种在不增加实质让步情况下提升接受性的方法。

Result: 被分类为社会谄媚程度更高的回复同时也更具接受性；提高人工回复的接受性会导致其被更强烈地判为社会谄媚；参与者更偏好更接受的回复，预期用户更愿意倾听，也更愿意向作者求助，即使认为提问者有错时该模式依然成立；所提方法能显著提升接受性而不增加实质让步。

Conclusion: 社会谄媚评估与对话接受性存在紧密耦合，可能无意中惩罚理想行为，构成构念效度风险；现有评估需区分社会接受性与实质让步；对话接受性与实质独立性可以同时实现。

Abstract: A central concern with language models is sycophancy: their tendency to defer to users' views at the expense of independent substantive judgment. In parallel, work on social sycophancy has focused on behaviors such as validation and positivity that may signal inappropriate deference. Yet the markers of social sycophancy are also characteristic of conversational receptiveness, a construct from social psychology shown to improve interactions across disagreement. We argue that this overlap creates a construct-validity problem for social sycophancy evaluations. Using a popular moral-advice dataset, we find that responses classified as more socially sycophantic are also more receptive. Further, increasing the receptiveness of human-written responses---while preserving their substantive conclusions---causes them to be classified as more socially sycophantic. This tight coupling raises the possibility that social sycophancy evaluations inadvertently penalize desirable behavior. In a preregistered experiment comparing substantively equivalent responses, participants prefer the more receptive responses, expect users to be more likely to listen to them, and are more willing to seek advice from their authors. The same overall pattern persists even among participants who believe the original question asker is in the wrong. Finally, we introduce a simple approach that substantially increases receptiveness without increasing substantive deference, demonstrating that conversational receptiveness and substantive independence can be achieved together.

</details>


### [178] [Semantic Abstraction for Natural Language Inference: a Methodological Framework for Discovering and Compensating Semantic Knowledge and Reasoning Gaps in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26610)
*David Torres-Moreno,Jorge Hermosillo-Valadez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个构建更高抽象层级语义知识的方法论框架，基于“语义兼容/不兼容”概念重构前提与假设之间的词汇-语义关系，形成更灵活的语义网络以诱导LLM的不同推理路径。实验表明该方法能发现并弥补LLM在NLI中的语义知识缺口，显著提升准确率（部分模型超过10%，尤其非蕴含类），并强调LLM需要结构化知识而非更多数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽在众多NLP任务上表现优异，但在语义抽象方面仍面临严峻挑战。作者关注LLM如何在自然语言推理（NLI）中利用抽象语义知识，因为这需要解读隐含意义、上下文概念关系及词与短语间语义联结的复杂语言能力。

Method: 提出一个在更高抽象层级上构建新语义知识的方法论框架，以“语义兼容性”与“语义不兼容性”定义于NLI语境中。在该框架下，重新配置前提与假设之间词汇-语义关系的含义，以形成更灵活的语义网络，从而诱导LLM产生不同推理路径；这些新路径呈现出一致的响应模式，使模型能够就单一响应达成一致（混合式方法）。

Result: 该方法能够发现并弥补LLM在NLI中的语义知识缺口，在准确率上取得显著提升，部分模型提升超过10%，在非蕴含（non-entailment）类别上尤为明显。

Conclusion: LLM需要结构化知识而非仅更多数据来弥合推理缺口。该混合方法引导关注被忽视的词间关系，使模型能综合缺失信息。作者认为未来不在扩大模型规模，而在于构建模仿人类思维灵活性的语义脚手架，从而推动更稳健的智能体与可解释推理的发展，导向可靠的语言理解。

Abstract: Despite their outstanding performance on many NLP tasks, LLMs face serious challenges related to semantic abstraction. In this study, we are interested in understanding how LLMs leverage abstract semantic knowledge in natural language inference (NLI), which requires sophisticated linguistic capabilities to interpret implicit meanings, contextual conceptual relationships, and semantic connections between words and phrases. To this end, we propose a methodological framework for constructing new semantic knowledge at a higher level of abstraction, which we define under the notions of semantic compatibility and incompatibility for NLI. In this framework, the meaning of the lexical-semantic relations between the premise and the hypothesis is reconfigured to achieve a more flexible semantic network that induces different reasoning paths in LLMs. These new pathways show a consistent pattern of responses that allows agreement on a single response. The results demonstrate that our proposal allows to discover and compensate for LLMs' semantic knowledge gaps in NLI, achieving significant improvements in accuracy, exceeding 10% for some models, and in particular for the non-entailment class. It is essential to note that LLMs need structured knowledge and not just more data to bridge reasoning gaps. Our hybrid approach directs attention to overlooked word relationships, allowing models to synthesize missing information. We believe that the future lies not in increasing model size, but in creating a semantic scafolding that mimics the flexibility of human thinking. Hopefully, our proposal will enable the development of more robust agents and interpretable reasoning, guiding AI toward reliable language understanding.

</details>


### [179] [PERSONAWEAVER: Controllable Diversity Beyond Conventional Archetypes in Procedural Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26629)
*Maan Qraitem,Kate Saenko,Bryan A. Plummer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 PersonaWeaver，通过将世界构建与行为规范解耦，利用人工整理的道德立场与对话反应库来指导 LLM 生成角色，从而缓解角色行为同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的程序化角色生成方法要么直接生成角色，要么复用角色库中的档案，导致生成的角色行为高度同质：普遍认同正面道德规范，回答问题时呈现助手式的乐于助人反应，缺乏多样性。

Method: PersonaWeaver 将世界构建与行为规范分离，通过设定通用、多样且人工整理的道德立场库与对话反应库，来显式建模角色行为，从而测试 LLM 在多大程度上能突破其默认行为模式。

Result: 在十个真实与奇幻设定及三个 LLM 上，PersonaWeaver 产生的道德与交互响应分布比先前工作更广；其引导还使角色间语言、回复长度和情感更加多样，并生成更少刻板化的世界属性组合。

Conclusion: 解耦世界构建与行为规范、并借助多样化人工整理的道德与对话库，能有效提升 LLM 生成角色的行为多样性，缓解同质化问题。

Abstract: Procedural character generation aims to populate games, simulations, and other virtual worlds with diverse characters. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising foundation for scaling this task. However, LLM-based procedural character generation remains at an early stage: existing methods either generate characters directly or adapt profiles retrieved from persona banks. As we show, both approaches produce behaviorally homogeneous populations: characters overwhelmingly agree with positive moral norms and respond to questions with helpful, assistant-like reactions. To mitigate this homogenization, we introduce PersonaWeaver, which disentangles world building from behavioral specification and models behavior through setting general, diverse, manually curated banks of moral positions and conversational reactions. This design allows us to test how far LLM(s) can be pushed beyond their default behavioral patterns across settings. Across ten realistic and fantastical settings and three LLM(s), PersonaWeaver produces broader moral and interactional response distributions than prior work. Its guidance also diversifies interpersonal language, response length, and sentiment. It also produces less archetypal combinations of world attributes. Code is available at https://github.com/mqraitem/PersonaWeaver.

</details>


### [180] [Knowledge Pull Requests for Continual Document Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26634)
*Alexander Martin,Benjamin Van Durme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Knowledge Pull Requests (KPRs) 框架，实现可解释的持续性文档写作：通过提取、过滤、路由 claim 并检测冲突，生成区分"知识变化"与"文本变化"的 ChangeLog。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档需随新知识（来自其他来源、语言或时间）持续修订，但现有方法要么在编辑时不交代知识变化，要么从头重新生成，缺乏可解释性与知识变更的显式追踪。

Method: KPR 通过提取 claim、过滤并将其路由到相应章节、标记与现有内容的冲突，将新知识整合进文档，并产出 ChangeLog，将"知识变化"（claim proposal）与"文本变化"（document diff）分离。

Result: 在跨语言修订 Wikipedia 和更新 RAGTIME 上查询驱动的报告任务中评估：KPR 比基于源重写或从头生成整合更多信息、更好保留原有内容，且单位生成 token 新增信息量最多；KPR 修订的文章在问答 grounding 上优于带搜索的前沿模型（后者无法呈现仅存在于其他语言的知识）。

Conclusion: KPR 提供了一种可解释、知识变更与文本变更解耦的持续性文档写作范式，在信息整合、内容保留、生成效率与问答支撑上均具优势。

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge Pull Requests (KPRs), a framework for continual document authoring that makes each change interpretable. Documents require ongoing revision as new knowledge surfaces from other sources, languages, or times, but existing approaches either edit with no account of what knowledge changed or regenerate from scratch. A KPR integrates new knowledge into a document by extracting claims, filtering and routing them to sections, and flagging conflicts with existing content, producing a ChangeLog that separates what knowledge changes (claim proposal) from how the text changes (document diff). We evaluate KPRs on revising Wikipedia across languages and updating query-driven reports on RAGTIME. KPRs integrate more information and better preserve existing content than rewriting from sources or regenerating from scratch, while adding the most information per token generated. A KPR-revised article also grounds question answering better than a frontier model with search, which does not surface knowledge documented only in other languages.

</details>


### [181] [Capable yet Parsimonious: Extracting and Characterizing Hidden Chain-of-Thought in Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26637)
*Xiaoyu Luo,Tao Ren,Wenrui Yu,Xiao Li,Qiongxiu Li,Johannes Bjerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过注册自定义工具诱导前沿模型外化中间推理，验证了提取出的推理轨迹与原生CoT性能相当，并据此刻画了前沿模型在token效率、推理步骤类型和推理树结构上的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型的推理能力提升被广泛归因于推理能力增强，但闭源系统中原始CoT轨迹被隐藏，导致这一归因无法被验证，因此需要一种能够从闭源前沿模型中提取并分析中间推理的方法。

Method: 通过标准API特性注册一个简单的自定义工具，诱导前沿模型将中间推理外化；为排除事后合理化（post-hoc rationalization）的干扰，先在开源模型上与原生CoT进行对照评估，再扩展到包括GPT-6 Astra在内的闭源前沿模型，并在竞赛数学、科学和代码生成任务上比较。随后从token效率、推理步骤类型和诱导推理树三个维度刻画模型组织推理的方式。

Result: 提取出的推理在性能上与原生推理持平，并显著优于无推理基线，覆盖竞赛数学、科学和代码生成任务；在不同模型间发现外化、压缩和组织推理方式的系统性差异；Astra展现出token高效的定向推理，更早选定正确轨迹，内部解决基础步骤，仅外化关键推理。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种超越基准分数的前沿模型推理行为视角，说明可通过工具调用诱导闭源模型外化推理并进行结构化分析。

Abstract: The rapid capability gains of frontier language models are widely attributed to improved reasoning abilities, yet this cannot be verified as raw CoT traces in closed-source systems are hidden. By registering a simple custom tool through a standard API feature, we induce frontier models to externalize intermediate reasoning. Because these traces may reflect post-hoc rationalization rather than genuine reasoning, we first evaluate against native CoT on open-source models and extend to closed-source frontier models including GPT-6 Astra. We find that the extracted reasoning matches native reasoning performance and substantially outperforms no-reasoning baselines, across competition mathematics, science, and code generation. We then characterize how frontier models structure their intermediate reasoning. Across token efficiency, reasoning-step types, and induced reasoning trees, we identify systematic differences in how models externalize, compress, and organize reasoning. We find that Astra exhibits token-efficient directed reasoning, selecting a correct trajectory earlier, while resolving elementary steps internally and externalizing only crucial reasoning. These findings provide a behavioral lens on frontier-model reasoning beyond benchmark scores.

</details>


### [182] [Diffusion Drafts, AR Verifies: Accelerating Document OCR with Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26638)
*Dohyun Kim,Sungjun Han,Hyungguk Kim,Yusik Kim,Jamin Shin,Paul Hongsuck Seo,Hongjoon Ahn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GravityOCR 是一种参数共享的自回归(AR)块扩散模型，联合训练并行草稿与因果 AR 验证，实现 OCR 多 token 并行提交，在保持精度的同时显著提升解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 OCR 视觉语言模型需逐 token 顺序解码，推理速度受限于每 token 一步解码。与开放式文本生成不同，OCR 输出与输入图像强绑定，使扩散式并行生成具备可行性；但一步预测多 token 时各 token 在彼此未知的情况下预测，直接提交会引入错误。

Method: 提出 GravityOCR，参数共享的 AR-块扩散模型，联合训练并行草稿与因果 AR 验证：先验证草稿再提交，无需独立草稿网络即可每轮提交多个 token；因果 AR 路径支持 GRPO，使用序列级与结构级 OCR 奖励，避免扩散轨迹似然估计，同时更新共享草稿器参数。

Result: 在 OmniDocBench v1.6 上，AR 路径 GRPO 将 Overall 分数从 94.92 提升至 95.16，且未降低扩散草稿效率，最终模型接近原始 GLM-OCR 的 95.48。SGLang 部署中平均每次前向提交 9.7 个输出 token，区域裁剪解码加速 3.94×，端到端页面处理较 AR 解码加速 1.32×。

Conclusion: 通过参数共享的 AR 块扩散与因果验证，GravityOCR 在不损失精度（接近 AR 基线）的前提下实现多 token 并行提交，显著提升 OCR 解码与端到端推理速度，验证了基于扩散的并行生成在强图像绑定任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Autoregressive OCR vision-language models accurately convert document images into text and structured markup, but require one sequential decoding step per output token, limiting inference speed. Unlike open-ended text generation, OCR outputs are strongly grounded in the input image, making diffusion-based parallel generation promising. However, when several tokens are predicted in one diffusion step, each is predicted before the others are known. Committing them directly can therefore introduce errors. We therefore introduce GravityOCR, a parameter-shared AR-block-diffusion model jointly trained for parallel drafting and causal AR verification. Verifying drafts before commitment lets the model commit multiple output tokens per round without a separate drafting network. The causal AR path also enables GRPO with sequence- and structure-level OCR rewards, avoiding diffusion-trajectory likelihood estimation while updating the shared drafter parameters. On OmniDocBench v1.6, AR-path GRPO improves the Overall score from 94.92 to 95.16 without reducing diffusion drafting efficiency, while the final model remains close to the original GLM-OCR score of 95.48. In an SGLang serving deployment, GravityOCR commits an average of 9.7 output tokens per forward pass and achieves a $3.94\times$ decode-only speedup on region crops and a $1.32\times$ end-to-end page-processing speedup over AR decoding.

</details>


### [183] [Detecting GPT-Assisted Writing Using Interpretable Stylometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26687)
*Rajesh Kumar,Nabeel Siddiqui,Alexander Fuchsberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估仅基于文本的风格计量特征在区分人工独立写作与GPT辅助写作上的判别力，使用90名参与者（同人内混合独立/辅助写作）的数据，比较八种机器学习分类器并按参与者分组验证，随机森林在测试集上取得ROC-AUC 0.87、F1 0.84。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术场景中区分学生独立写作与GPT辅助写作已成为关键难题，需要可解释、仅依赖提交文本的检测信号。

Method: 采用可解释的风格计量特征，仅从提交文本中提取；数据来自90名参与者，每人既有独立写作也有ChatGPT辅助写作；评估八种机器学习分类器，验证时保持同一参与者的数据在同一折内；用SHAP分析解释特征贡献。

Result: 留出测试集上随机森林ROC-AUC为0.87、F1为0.84，假阳性率22.2%、假阴性率11.1%；SHAP显示词汇与语法特征主导预测。

Conclusion: 透明的、文本内在的风格计量特征能为检测GPT辅助写作提供可测量的信号。

Abstract: Distinguishing GPT-assisted from independently authored student writing has become a critical challenge in academia. This paper evaluates the discriminative capability of interpretable stylometric features extracted solely from submitted text. Using data from 90 participants who wrote both independently and with ChatGPT assistance, we evaluate eight machine learning classifiers while keeping data from the same participant together during validation. On the held-out test set, Random Forest achieved an ROC-AUC of 0.87 and an F1-score of 0.84, with False Positive and False Negative rates of 22.2% and 11.1%, respectively. SHAP analysis shows that lexical and grammatical characteristics drive the resulting predictions. The findings suggest that transparent, text-intrinsic features provide measurable signal for detecting GPT-assisted writing.

</details>


### [184] [Measuring the Serving Stack Instead of the Model: Hidden Confounds in Local Tool-Use Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26693)
*Lijuan Tang,Yuemeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 编码智能体必须先输出可解析的工具调用，本文发现该协议步骤受本地服务栈影响：Ollama 按模板标志对工具请求做静态门控（部分模型被接受、部分被拒），拒绝与重试耗尽未被保留为结构化失败元数据，导致下游将"非模型调用"误判为 0% 保真度；跨栈（Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang）处理同一请求方式不同，约束解码消除解析失败但可引发不终止，轮次池化与逐实例估计差异可达约 55 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用是智能体执行动作的前置协议步骤，但既有评测常把结果归因于模型行为，忽略了本地服务栈这一混淆变量，可能导致错误的保真度结论与跨模型比较。

Method: 在自建 harness 中考察 Ollama 对 tools= 请求的按模型静态模板门控（接受/以文本返回调用/返回原生 tool_calls/推理前拒绝），并对比加入文本工具列表与统一文本协议的效果；在 Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang 上做跨栈探测，并比较约束解码与轮次池化/逐实例两种估计方式。

Result: Phi-3 与 Gemma-3 在推理前即被拒绝；拒绝与重试耗尽未记为结构化失败，易被误分类为模型非调用并报出 0% 保真度。保留原生通道并附加文本工具列表可大幅恢复被接受模型的测得保真度，而统一文本协议会降低本就支持原生调用的 Llama-3.2 的保真度。同一请求在不同服务栈处理方式不同；约束解码消除解析失败但会诱导不终止；轮次池化与逐实例估计差异最高约 55 个百分点。

Conclusion: 服务行为应被视为评测协议的一部分，需将服务层门控、失败类型元数据、解码约束与估计口径纳入报告，作者据此给出检查清单。

Abstract: A coding agent must emit a valid tool call--a parseable invocation of a tool in the provided schema--before the harness can execute its chosen action. We study how local serving stacks affect this protocol step and show that measured outcomes can depend on the serving layer rather than model behavior alone. In Ollama, the default tools= request is gated per model by a static template flag: some models are accepted and return calls as text, some return native tool_calls, while Phi-3 and Gemma-3 are rejected before inference. In our harness, rejection and retry exhaustion are not preserved as structured failure metadata, so downstream analysis can misclassify them as model non-calls and naively report 0% fidelity. Adding a text tool list while retaining the native channel recovers much of the measured fidelity for accepted models, whereas a uniform text protocol reduces fidelity for Llama-3.2, which has native tool-call support. Cross-stack probes on Ollama, llama.cpp, vLLM, and SGLang show different handling of the same request. Constrained decoding removes parse failures but can induce non-termination, and turn-pooled versus per-instance estimates differ by up to about 55 points. We conclude with a checklist for treating serving behavior as part of the evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Repeated Sampling: Learning Search Policies for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26704)
*Ismail Labiad,Matthieu Kowalski,Marc Schoenauer,Rémi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在语义层面引导大模型的测试时探索：先生成问题相关的概念/提示/策略，再据此条件化答案生成，并用强化学习将小型"概念生成器"训练成可复用的搜索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升测试时计算的主要策略是朴素重复采样，但采样只通过局部解码噪声进行探索，容易产生大量近乎重复的尝试，而非真正不同的思路，因此在困难推理问题上效果受限。

Method: 首先提出一种简单、更具探索性的过程，在单条轨迹中生成大量多样化概念，并在重复采样表现不佳的难题上评估；进而将概念生成变为可训练：用强化学习优化一个小的概念生成器，使其生成的概念最大化下游冻结的大答案生成器的成功概率。

Result: 在困难数学推理问题上，训练后的概念生成器在相同答案生成预算下显著提升了答案生成器的pass@k，优于朴素重复采样；其生成的概念超过来自规模大得多但未调优的模型的概念；并且可迁移到未参与训练的答案生成器，包括不同家族的模型。

Conclusion: 小模型可被训练为面向更大模型的有效且可复用的搜索策略，说明在语义层面引导测试时探索比单纯增加采样量更有价值。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly tackle hard reasoning problems by spending more test-time compute, yet the dominant strategy remains naive repeated sampling: draw many independent solutions and hope one is correct. Because such sampling explores only through local decoding noise, it tends to produce many near duplicate attempts rather than genuinely different ideas. We ask whether exploration can instead be steered at a semantic level, by first sampling problem specific concepts, hints, or strategies and then conditioning answer generation on them. We refine this into a simple, more exploratory procedure that emits many diverse concepts in a single trajectory, and evaluate it on hard problems where repeated sampling struggles. We then go a step further and make concept generation trainable: a small concept generator is optimized with reinforcement learning so that its concepts maximize the downstream success of a larger, frozen answer generator. On hard mathematical reasoning problems, the trained concept generator substantially improves the answer generator's pass@k over naive repeated sampling at the same answer generation allocation, surpasses concepts drawn from much larger untuned models, and transfers to answer generators it was never trained against, including a model from a different family. A small model can thus be trained into an effective, reusable search policy for a much larger one.

</details>


### [186] [SpeakerMem-R1: Speaker-Centered Dual-Track Memory for Multi-Party Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26780)
*Haobo Zheng,Tan Tang,Yan Chen,Weijie Wang,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SpeakerMem-R1，通过双轨记忆（逐字消息 + 结构化状态，含人物级/群体级视图）与 RL 训练的 Writer-R1，解决多方对话中的消息归属、关系理解与状态重建难题，在多个基准上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方场景下的长期对话记忆不仅需要检索相关内容，还必须区分"谁说了什么"、语句指向谁、个体间相互认知、群体共享信息以及状态随时间的变化。现有多方对话基准显示，通用 LLM 记忆系统易丢失人物与群体关系，或难以整合分散在成员、群体与时间中的线索。这暴露出两个核心瓶颈：多方对话中的消息归属与关系理解，以及从交错历史中重建状态。

Method: 提出 SpeakerMem-R1：1）双轨记忆，存储带说话人标注的逐字消息与派生状态，并组织为人物级和群体级视图；查询时按实体、事件、时间融合两条轨道的证据。2）为降低结构化记忆构建中的归属与更新错误并支持本地部署，使用 SpeakerLevenshtein 与 speaker-conditioned GRPO 训练 Writer-R1。

Result: 在 GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench、EverMemBench 上二分类准确率分别为 47.9%、69.2%、61.9%；在 EverMind-AI 公开 EverMemBench 榜单上达 62.33%，为最新 SOTA 框架中最佳。在全部 1,986 道 LoCoMo 问题上达 70.85%（作为双人长期对话边界测试）。在 305 题的受控评测中，RL 将 SFT Writer 的平均准确率从 57.38% 提升至 68.20%。同时报告二分类准确率与 token-F1。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，逐字轨道与结构化轨道，以及人物级与群体级视图，在标准化评测接口下具有互补性。SpeakerMem-R1 有效缓解了多方对话记忆中的归属与状态重建瓶颈，并在多项基准上优于现有最新框架。

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory in multi-party settings requires more than retrieving relevant content from long-term conversations: it must distinguish who said what, whom each statement concerns, how individuals perceive one another, what information is shared by the group, and how states change over time. Recent studies on multi-party dialogue benchmarks show that existing general-purpose LLM memory systems tend to lose person and group relations or struggle to integrate clues distributed across members, groups, and time. Together, these issues reveal two core bottlenecks: message attribution and relational understanding in multi-party dialogue, and state reconstruction from interleaved histories. To address both, we propose $\textbf{SpeakerMem-R1}$: its dual-track memory stores speaker-labeled verbatim messages and derived states organized into person-level and group-level views, then combines evidence from both tracks by entity, event, and time at query time. To reduce attribution and update errors during structured memory construction while enabling local deployment, we train Writer-R1 with SpeakerLevenshtein and speaker-conditioned GRPO. On GroupMemBench, SocialMemBench, and EverMemBench, SpeakerMem-R1 achieves binary accuracies of 47.9%, 69.2%, and 61.9%, respectively. On the publicly reported EverMemBench leaderboard from EverMind-AI, we achieves 62.33%, the best reported result among the latest state-of-the-art frameworks. It also achieves 70.85% on all 1,986 LoCoMo questions, which we use as a two-person long-term conversation boundary test. In a controlled evaluation of 305 questions, RL raises the SFT Writer's mean accuracy from 57.38% to 68.20%. We report both binary accuracy and token-F1, and ablations show that the verbatim and structured tracks, as well as person-level and group-level views, are complementary under the standardized evaluation interface.

</details>


### [187] [Agensh: Scaling Organizational Intelligence to 1,024 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26781)
*Zhihao Zhan,Ting Song,Li Dong,Shaohan Huang,Jianxun Lian,Yan Xia,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agensh，一种无可中心编排器的自组织多智能体执行框架（harness），通过并发 worker 执行"上下文收集—任务认领—行动—验证—合并"的异步协作循环实现横向扩展，在 ProgramBench 与 pandoc 上验证了智能体数量可作为新的扩展维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多种多智能体 harness 框架可并行处理复杂任务以降低延迟，但其可扩展性受限于中心编排器（central orchestrator）在任务分配与 worker 协调上的容量瓶颈。因此需要一种去除中心编排、可支持大规模智能体自组织协作的框架。

Method: 提出 Agensh：无可中心编排器的可扩展自组织多智能体 harness。并发 worker 以异步方式执行多智能体协作循环，流程为持续收集上下文、认领并自我分配子任务、执行动作并共享发现、验证结果、合并进展。该循环依托由三部分构成的 agentic organization infrastructure 支撑：共享工作空间（存放已提议、进行中与已完成的工作）、消息接口（worker 间通信）、共享上下文（保留可复用的发现与工作意图）。评测在 ProgramBench 中最难的五个任务上使用 GPT-5.6-sol (high) 进行，并额外在 pandoc 上做更大规模扩展实验。

Result: 在 ProgramBench 五个最难任务上，智能体数量从 1 扩展到 128 时，平均最终测试通过率由 19.31% 提升至 28.78%，相对提升约 49%；更大规模的组织能更早达到相当的通过率。在 pandoc 上，从 1 扩展到 1,024 个智能体，最终测试通过率由 33.89% 提升至 55.06%。worker 轨迹显示，随着组织规模增长，不同形式的自组织协作逐渐涌现并趋于标准化。

Conclusion: 智能体数量是多智能体组织扩展通用智能前沿的一个新扩展维度（new scaling dimension）。Agensh 为受严格延迟约束或时间预算限制的复杂任务提供了实用方案，并表明去中心化自组织协作可在规模增长下自发形成并被规范化。

Abstract: A multi-agent system can reduce latency on complex tasks by executing work concurrently. Several pioneering harness frameworks support multi-agent systems. However, the scalability of current multi-agent harnesses is often constrained by a central orchestrator's capacity to allocate tasks and coordinate workers. To address this limitation, we introduce Agensh, a scalable self-organized multi-agent harness without a central orchestrator: concurrent workers execute a multi-agent cooperation loop, continuously gathering context, claiming and self-assigning sub-tasks, taking action and sharing findings, verifying results, and merging progress in an asynchronous manner. The loop is supported by the agentic organization infrastructure comprising three components: a shared workspace holds proposed, ongoing, and completed work; a message interface lets workers communicate; and shared context retains reusable findings and work intentions. To test the scalability of Agensh, we evaluate it on the five hardest ProgramBench tasks with GPT-5.6-sol (high). Scaling from 1 to 128 agents raises the mean final test-pass rate from 19.31% to 28.78%, an approximately 49% relative improvement. Larger organizations reach comparable test-pass rates earlier. On pandoc, scaling from 1 to 1,024 agents raises the final test-pass rate from 33.89% to 55.06%. Worker trajectories further show that different forms of self-organized cooperation gradually emerges and standardizes as the organization grows. These results reveal the number of agents as a new scaling dimension for multi-agent organizations to expand the frontier of general intelligence, offering a practical solution for complex tasks under hard latency constraints or time budgets.

</details>


### [188] [Flash-dLLM: IO-Aware KV Caching and Parallel Decoding for Fast, Memory-Efficient Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26796)
*Quan Nguyen-Tri,Mukul Ranjan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Flash-dLLM，一个无需训练的扩散大语言模型（dLLM）推理加速框架，通过 I/O 感知的融合 KV 缓存内核与 KV 缓存驱动的自草稿-自验证解码策略，同时优化内存 I/O 与并行解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）虽支持非自回归文本生成，但推理效率低，主要缺乏有效的 KV 缓存与可扩展的并行解码机制。现有加速方法通常将 KV 缓存与并行解码割裂研究，忽视了二者联合应用时产生的 I/O 瓶颈。

Method: Flash-dLLM 为免训练框架：首先识别出 GPU 内存 I/O 是启用 KV 缓存后 dLLM 推理的主要瓶颈，并设计 I/O 感知的融合 KV 缓存内核以减少冗余内存搬运；随后在该优化缓存机制上提出 KV 缓存驱动的草稿-验证解码策略，由 dLLM 自身同时充当草稿器与验证器，无需辅助模型。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成基准上，Flash-dLLM 在推理速度与内存效率上均持续优于现有最先进的 dLLM 加速方法。在 GSM8K 与 HumanEval 上分别较最强基线 Elastic-Cache 实现 5.1× 和 11.0× 加速。

Conclusion: 通过统一优化 KV 缓存 I/O 与并行解码，Flash-dLLM 在不损失生成质量的前提下显著提升解码速度，并改善对长序列与大 batch 的可扩展性，为 dLLM 的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs by enabling non-autoregressive text generation. However, their practical deployment remains limited by inefficient inference, largely due to the absence of effective Key-Value (KV) caching and scalable parallel decoding mechanisms. Existing acceleration methods typically study KV caching and parallel decoding in isolation, overlooking the I/O bottlenecks that arise when cache reuse and parallel token verification are jointly applied. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{Flash-dLLM}$, a training-free inference acceleration framework for fast and memory-efficient dLLMs. Flash-dLLM first identifies GPU memory I/O as a dominant bottleneck in KV-cache-enabled dLLM inference and addresses it with an I/O-aware fused KV-cache kernel that reduces redundant memory movement. Building on this optimized cache mechanism, Flash-dLLM further proposes an efficient KV-cache-driven draft-and-verify decoding strategy, where the dLLM itself serves as both drafter and verifier without requiring an auxiliary model. This unified design enables faster decoding while preserving generation quality and improving scalability to longer sequences and larger batch size. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that Flash-dLLM consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art dLLM acceleration methods in both inference speed and memory efficiency. In particular, it achieves $5.1\times$ and $11.0\times$ speedups over prior strongest baseline Elastic-Cache on GSM8K and HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以 Upworthy Research Archive 的真实标题 A/B 测试为留出真值，开展 sim-to-real 效度研究，发现"合成人格"（persona）条件化反而降低预测效度：无人格零样本基线显著优于十人人格面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销者日益使用大语言模型作为"合成人格"来预测受众对文案的反应，其依据是"带画像条件的 LLM 能模仿人类样本"的证据。但这一预测对真实行为是否有效、人格机制是否有帮助，尚缺乏严格验证，本文正是针对该效度缺口展开研究。

Method: 以 Upworthy Research Archive（数千个共享真实流量上的标题 A/B 测试，含实测点击率）作为留出真值。对比两种方法：(1) 基于真实受众人口统计特征的十人人格面板；(2) 无人格的零样本基线，直接询问模型"典型读者点击的可能性"。在可信子集（n=399）上测量效度，并做稳健性检验。

Result: 真值可靠性是约束瓶颈：多数 A/B 测试无可统计区分的胜出者，故效度只能在可靠子集（n=399）上测量。与人格模拟前提相反，人格条件化会损害预测效度：无人格基线排序明显更好（Kendall τ=0.361，中等效应；top-1 准确率 49.2%），优于人格面板（τ=0.084；top-1 34.6%），且置信区间不重叠。结果在三个独立 Upworthy 划分上复现，在另一领域新闻数据集上方向一致，并对随机种子、提示措辞和模型选择稳健（三个 Gemini 层级及另一模型家族 OpenAI gpt-4.1，配对差距显著）。

Conclusion: 在预测总体参与度时，朴素的 LLM 排序器优于人格模拟；合成人格不仅是弱预测器，甚至比不使用更差。直接询问模型可获取准确的人群层面先验，而强制角色扮演具体人格会引入偏差与噪声。所有数值均可由公开的 artifact-first 复现包重新生成。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [190] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了五种基于AdamW的预条件策略在TabPFN v2.5生物医学表格数据微调中的效果，发现原始AdamW在59个数据集上整体表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（如TabPFN）在结构化生物医学数据分析中展现出潜力，但优化与预条件策略对生物医学微调的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对五种基于AdamW的预条件策略在TabPFN v2.5上进行全面实证研究，覆盖59个生物医学数据集（含阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、SMC、KEEL及UCI基准），并从预测性能、计算效率和统计显著性三方面评估。

Result: 原始AdamW优化器在整体性能和统计排名上始终最优；现有曲率感知预条件器在多样生物医学学习场景中未能提供可靠提升。

Conclusion: 通用预条件方法难以充分捕捉生物医学表格学习的优化特性，需要针对医疗导向表格基础模型开发生物医学感知的预条件器。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis. Among them, TabPFN has emerged as an effective approach for low-data tabular classification tasks. However, the impact of optimization and preconditioning strategies on biomedical fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical investigation of five AdamW-based preconditioning strategies for fine-tuning TabPFN v2.5 on 59 biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease, breast cancer, schizophrenia, significant memory concern (SMC), KEEL biomedical datasets, and UCI biomedical benchmarks. The evaluation considers predictive performance, computational efficiency, and statistical significance analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that the original AdamW optimizer consistently achieves the best overall performance and statistical ranking, while existing curvature-aware preconditioners fail to provide reliable improvements across diverse biomedical learning scenarios. The findings suggest that generic preconditioning approaches may not adequately capture the optimization characteristics of biomedical tabular learning, motivating the development of biomedical-aware preconditioners specifically tailored for healthcare-oriented tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [191] [4DGS-JEPA: Temporally Compositional Joint-Embedding Prediction for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25036)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 4DGS-JEPA 提出一种高斯原生的联合嵌入预测架构，以分层表示与时序组合原则实现动态高斯场景的因果多时域预测，并配以几何解码与混合对应机制，通过三个受控实验验证其机制有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅方法主要面向重建、未来状态生成或渲染，未针对"学习可复用的预测动力学"进行优化，缺乏对多时域预测与动力学可复用性的显式建模。

Method: 采用场景级、运动组级、高斯级的分层表示，以及时域条件转移算子，支持直接预测与递归展开；核心原则为时序组合——不同时间转移路径到达同一未来端点应产生兼容的预测状态。监督包含端点与多时域路径监督，并以选择性几何解码器与几何级组合将动力学 grounding 于组运动与高斯几何，避免完整的未来外观重建。此外引入混合对应机制，结合持久规范身份与残差最优传输匹配，以应对重排序与拓扑变化。

Result: 理论上刻画了零损失下的路径一致性与有限误差下的展开累积特性。三个受控实验提供机制层面证据：时序组合降低潜空间路径依赖且保留预测精度；几何级组合提升解码运动的一致性；混合对应机制在保持可靠身份的同时，在对应模糊时仍具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 4DGS-JEPA 为动态高斯世界提供了一种具备预测性与时序组合性的建模范式。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation of evolving 3D scenes, but existing approaches are primarily optimized for reconstruction, future-state generation, or rendering rather than for learning reusable predictive dynamics. We propose 4DGS-JEPA, a Gaussian-native joint-embedding predictive architecture for causal multi-horizon prediction over dynamic Gaussian scenes. The model uses a hierarchical scene-, motion-group-, and Gaussian-level representation together with a horizon-conditioned transition operator that supports both direct prediction and recursive rollout. Its central principle is temporal composition: different chronological transition paths reaching the same future endpoint should produce compatible predictive states. Endpoint and multi-horizon path supervision anchor these predictions to future target embeddings, while a selective geometry decoder and geometry-level composition ground the learned dynamics in consistent group motion and Gaussian geometry without requiring complete future appearance reconstruction. We further introduce a hybrid correspondence mechanism that combines persistent canonical identity with residual optimal-transport matching under reordering and topology change. We characterize zero-loss path agreement and finite-error rollout accumulation theoretically. Three controlled experiments provide mechanism-level evidence that temporal composition reduces latent path dependence while retaining predictive accuracy, geometry-level composition improves consistency of decoded motion, and hybrid correspondence preserves reliable identity while remaining robust when correspondence becomes ambiguous. Together, 4DGS-JEPA provides a predictive, temporally compositional formulation of dynamic Gaussian worlds.

</details>


### [192] [An Accurate and Interpretable Hyper Graph Neural Network for GBM Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25088)
*Mushahid Intesum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出融合sheaf超图神经网络、概念瓶颈层与扩展充分性检验(EST)正则的多模态框架，在UPenn-GBM 593例上实现C-index 0.643、标准差仅0.015的可解释生存预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质母细胞瘤(GBM)生存预测要求模型同时具备准确性与可解释性，但现有方法将二者视为竞争关系：高精度模型牺牲透明度，可解释模型牺牲预测性能。作者认为该权衡并非固有，并指出当前方法多依赖单一影像模态，未充分利用多模态MRI与临床元数据的互补信息。

Method: 提出多模态框架，含三个核心组件：(1) sheaf超图神经网络，通过有向、非对称消息传递捕捉组织patch间高阶关系；(2) 概念瓶颈层，将学习表征压缩为临床上有依据的概念，实现先验(ex ante)可解释性；(3) 扩展充分性检验(EST)正则项，在训练中惩罚不忠实的解释，确保解释真实反映内部决策过程。临床与基因组特征通过门控融合引入，保留分子标志物的主导预后信号并维持概念级可追溯性。

Result: 在UPenn-GBM数据集593例患者上以5折交叉验证评估，框架达到一致性指数(C-index)0.643，且在所有对比模型中折间方差最低(std = 0.015)。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个统一sheaf超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与EST正则用于脑MRI可解释生存预测的工作，论证了准确性与可解释性可通过结构化的图方法协同实现而非相互竞争。

Abstract: Survival prediction for glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) demands models that are both accurate and interpretable, yet existing approaches treat these objectives as com- peting, where performant models sacrifice transparency, while interpretable models accept degraded predictive power. We argue that this trade-off is not inherent. Graph neural net- works offer a structural foundation for extracting interpretable, explainable representations without compromising discriminative ability. Furthermore, current methods typically rely on a single imaging modality, underutilizing the complementary information available across multi-modal MRI and clinical metadata. We propose a multi-modal framework that inte- grates three components to address both objectives simultaneously: (1) a sheaf hypergraph neural network that captures higher-order relationships among tissue patches through direc- tional, asymmetric message passing; (2) a concept bottleneck layer that compresses learned representations into clinically grounded concepts, enforcing ante-hoc interpretability; and (3) an extension sufficiency test (EST) regularizer that penalizes unfaithful explanations during training, ensuring that model explanations genuinely reflect the internal decision process. Clinical and genomic features are incorporated through gated fusion, preserving the dominant prognostic signal of molecular markers while retaining concept-level traceabil- ity. Evaluated on 593 patients from the UPenn-GBM dataset under 5-fold cross-validation, our framework achieves a concordance index of 0.643 with the lowest fold-level variance among all compared models (std = 0.015). To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify sheaf hypergraph convolution, concept bottleneck supervision, and EST regularization for interpretable survival prediction from brain MRI

</details>


### [193] [Ovis-Embedding: Pushing the Frontiers of Universal Omni-Modal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25165)
*Ovis-Embedding Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Ovis-Embedding全模态嵌入模型族，基于共享多模态骨干统一编码文本、图像、视频与音频，在MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB、RTEB等基准上取得SOTA，验证统一全模态训练对任意模态检索的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型多采用独立模态塔（separate modality towers）的拼接式架构，导致模态割裂（modality fragmentation），难以在统一表示空间中实现跨模态、任意到任意（any-to-any）检索。作者希望用原生集成的共享多模态骨干替代模块化拼装，以提升跨模态对齐与泛化能力。

Method: 1）原生全模态初始化：以预训练Qwen-omni为嵌入骨干，通过低秩初始化（low-rank initialization）进行对比训练适配；2）数据中心化全模态训练：构建覆盖文本、图像、视频、音频及交错多模态数据的高质量语料，并引入同源采样（homogeneous-source sampling）构造任务一致批次，获得有信息量的批内负样本；3）嵌入专属训练与推理优化：训练端使用focal loss强化难例，并借助基于相似度的Embedding Distillation从互补专家模型中迁移细粒度相似度结构；推理端采用低秩特征分解，实现维度灵活且性能损失极小的紧凑嵌入。

Result: Ovis-Embedding系列在MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB和RTEB五个基准上均达到state-of-the-art性能，覆盖文本、图像、视频、音频模态，证明其对全模态检索任务的有效性；同时低秩特征分解支持灵活维度的紧凑嵌入且性能损失极小。

Conclusion: 统一的全模态训练能够有效克服模态割裂问题，推动通用嵌入模型向任意到任意检索发展，Ovis-Embedding为此提供了可行且性能领先的范式。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce \textbf{Ovis-Embedding}, a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding family built on native integration of text, image, video, and audio. Instead of assembling separate modality towers, Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone to encode different modalities in a common representation space. Specifically, we make \textbf{three key advances}: (1) \textbf{native omni-modal initialization}: we adopt a pretrained Qwen-omni model as the embedding backbone and adapt it through contrastive training with low-rank initialization; (2) \textbf{data-centric omni-modal training}: we construct a broad, high-quality corpus spanning text, images, video, audio, and interleaved multimodal data. To improve data efficiency, we introduce homogeneous-source sampling to form task-consistent batches with informative in-batch negatives; and (3) \textbf{embedding-specific training and inference optimization}: we use focal loss to emphasize hard examples and similarity-based Embedding Distillation to transfer fine-grained similarity structure from complementary experts. At inference time, low-rank feature decomposition enables compact embeddings with flexible dimensionality and minimal performance loss. Empirical evaluations show that the \textbf{Ovis-Embedding} family achieves state-of-the-art performance on \textbf{MMEB-v3}, \textbf{MMEB-v2}, \textbf{MVEB}, \textbf{MAEB}, and \textbf{RTEB}, demonstrating its effectiveness across text, image, video, and audio modalities. These results highlight the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality fragmentation and advance universal embedding models for any-to-any retrieval.

</details>


### [194] [X-Planner: Event-Structured Task Planning for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25187)
*Howard Lu,Shalfun Li,Porter Pan,Cris,Lumen,Cyril,Eric Hu,Lily Li,Maeve Zhang,Robert Wang,KZ Zheng,Viggo Chen,Tim Ding,Regsis Cheng,YJ Xiao,Kian,Hai Lin,Alan Song,Elise Ma,Gody Li,Victor Yao,Yohann Tang,Ingrid Yu,Jason He,James Wang,Ryan Yu,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Vincent Chen,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-Planner是一种规划前端，通过层次化标注数据与事件结构化的离散/潜在双接口建模，改进长程操作中VLA系统的具身推理监督与表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程操作中任务规划连接高层指令与可执行行为，但现有VLA系统将这一中间结构隐式化；现有CoT规划器依赖粗粒度任务级标注或逐token序列化长推理链，导致监督与表示不足。

Method: 数据上将Ego、UMI与遥操作按层次粒度组织，采用源依赖的标注深度，并用takeover-time标注与人工设计失败案例监督进行中的错误识别；模型侧共享VLM主干提供两种事件结构化规划形式：输出可解释事件状态的离散接口，以及通过Staircase Decoding在交错Transformer深度间传递连续CoT状态的潜在接口，并以冻结的潜在到文本重建目标作为语义锚点。

Result: 离线两步规划评估中，X-Planner在BERTScore-F1与基于judge的Overall得分上于四个被评模型中均列第二；真实机器人实验中分别优于所评估的基线。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了规划文本质量与下游执行表现，表明事件结构化规划前端在具身推理的监督与表示方面具有有效性。

Abstract: Task planning bridges high-level instructions and executable behavior in long-horizon manipulation, yet modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems often leave this intermediate structure implicit. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) planners also tend to rely on coarse task-level annotations or serialize long reasoning traces token by token. We present X-Planner, a planning front-end that addresses both the supervision and representation of embodied reasoning. Our planning data combine Ego, UMI, and teleoperation under a hierarchy granularity with source-dependent annotation depth. Takeover-time annotations and human-designed failures supervise ongoing error recognition. On the model side, a shared VLM backbone exposes two event-structured plan forms: a discrete interface that emits interpretable event states and a latent interface that relays continuous CoT states across staggered Transformer depths through Staircase Decoding. A frozen latent-to-text reconstruction objective provides a semantic anchor for the latent representation. Offline two-step planning evaluation places X-Planner second among four evaluated models on both BERTScore-F1 and a judge-based Overall score. In real-robot experiments, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results characterize planning-text quality and downstream execution.

</details>


### [195] [VideoX-Qwen: Data-Centric Instruction-Based Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26015)
*JJiahang Li,Dingbao Shao,Xinyu Chen,Song Wu,Jiang Lin,Duo Li,Yuhang Liu,Jiaxin Hu,Shengrong Gu,Ying Tai,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VideoX-Qwen，一套面向通用指令视频编辑的数据构建与模型训练一体化框架：构建超120万条成对视频编辑数据（自动接受率89%），并训练统一Qwen-Wan编辑器，在11项指标中9项取得最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视频编辑的进展依赖大规模成对监督数据以及将视频生成骨干有效适配到指令驱动编辑。与视频生成不同，视频编辑需在执行指定变换的同时保持无关主体、场景结构、运动与时间连续性不变，因此对数据规模、结构性与模型适配都提出更高要求。

Method: 数据侧：设计可扩展生产流水线，将专用生成模型与理解模型组织为互补路径，覆盖添加、移除、替换、属性编辑四类任务，经质量筛选与指令增强，产出超120万条有向视频编辑记录（各主要任务组均超40万条），以"源-指令-目标"统一接口组织。模型侧：构建统一的Qwen-Wan编辑器，融合多模态语义条件与稠密源视频潜变量引导；采用渐进式图像-视频训练策略，依次对齐多模态指令接口、将视频生成器适配为源条件编辑、并用精选高分辨率数据精修输出质量。

Result: 在与UniVideo和Kling O1的100例对比中，VideoX-Qwen在11项报告指标中的9项取得最佳均值，涵盖指令遵循、编辑质量、内容保持、结构与感知相似度以及视频分布质量。

Conclusion: 大规模数据生产系统与统一训练框架相结合，为更强的指令驱动视频编辑提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Progress in general-purpose video editing depends on constructing large-scale paired supervision and effectively adapting video-generation backbones to instruction-driven editing. Unlike video generation, video editing must execute a requested transformation while preserving unrelated subjects, scene structure, motion, and temporal continuity. We present VideoX-Qwen, an integrated data-construction and model-training framework for general instruction-based video editing. Our scalable production pipeline organizes specialized generation and understanding models into complementary routes for addition, removal, replacement, and attribute editing, followed by quality screening and instruction enrichment. It produces more than 1.2 million directional video-editing records, including over 400,000 records in each major task group, with an automatic acceptance rate of 89%. The resulting corpus provides broad and structured coverage of common editing operations through a unified source-instruction-target interface. We further develop a unified Qwen-Wan editor that combines multimodal semantic conditioning with dense source-video latent guidance. A progressive image-video training strategy aligns the multimodal instruction interface, adapts the video generator to source-conditioned editing, and refines output quality with selected high-resolution data. In a 100-example comparison with UniVideo and Kling O1, VideoX-Qwen achieves the best mean result on nine of eleven reported metrics, including instruction following, editing quality, content preservation, structural and perceptual similarity, and video-distribution quality. Together, the large-scale data-production system and unified training framework provide a practical foundation for more capable instruction-driven video editing.

</details>


### [196] [Lean Pool: An AI-Maintained Archive of Formalized Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25199)
*Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Lean Pool：一个由 AI 智能体负责培育、维护与优化的形式化数学仓库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学（如 Lean 中的数学库）长期依赖人工编写与维护，成本高、扩展速度受限，难以快速规模化积累可复用的形式化成果；因此探索以 AI 智能体自动驱动仓库增长与优化成为动机。

Method: 构建名为 Lean Pool 的仓库，将其定位为形式化数学的集合，并由 AI 智能体承担内容的生成（增长）、维护（一致性、修复）与优化（重构、复用、性能）等持续循环的工作。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验或量化指标，仅陈述该系统/仓库的定位与运行方式，即由 AI 智能体实现自主式的形式化数学积累与迭代。

Conclusion: Lean Pool 代表一种 AI 自主构建与演进形式化数学知识库的新范式，未来可望降低人工成本并加速形式化数学规模扩展。

Abstract: Lean Pool is a repository of formalized mathematics. It is grown, maintained and optimized by AI agents.

</details>


### [197] [The AI Neuroscientist: An Interactive Agentic Interface for Neuroimaging Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25254)
*Aakash Patel,Panos Ketonis,Shreya Saxena,Smita Krishnaswamy,David van Dijk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AI Neuroscientist——一个集成大语言模型与神经影像工具集的语言智能体，支持以自然语言进行数据质控、建模和可视化，面向小规模探索性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像数据分析需要专业的编程与统计能力，对缺乏计算背景的研究者构成门槛，限制了分析的可及性。

Method: 将大语言模型（LLM）与神经影像工具集集成，构建可交互的语言智能体，实现质量控制、建模与可视化；研究者可用自然语言查询数据质量并指定分析参数。基于功能近红外光谱（fNIRS）数据进行能力演示，并在自建 fNIRS 基准测试套件上，与配备代码沙箱的通用 LLM 智能体进行比较评估。

Result: 在 fNIRS 数据上展示了质控、建模与可视化能力；并在定制 fNIRS 基准套件中相对通用 LLM 智能体完成评估（具体量化结果未在摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: 该智能体为小规模数据探索提供了透明、可交互的替代方案；未来将把架构推广到功能磁共振成像（fMRI）等更多模态，并扩展基准测试任务。

Abstract: Analyzing neuroimaging data requires specialized coding and statistical expertise, which limits accessibility for researchers without computational backgrounds. We present the AI Neuroscientist, a language agent for interactive data exploration. The system integrates a large language model (LLM) with a neuroimaging toolset to perform quality control, modeling, and visualization. This allows researchers to query data quality and specify analysis parameters directly in natural language, providing a transparent and interactive alternative to conventional scripted pipelines for small-scale data exploration. We demonstrate these capabilities using functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) data, and evaluate the agent on a custom fNIRS benchmarking suite against general-purpose LLM agents with code sandboxes. Future extensions will generalize the architecture to additional modalities, including functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, and expand the benchmarking suite to additional fNIRS tasks.

</details>


### [198] [MedGate-Fusion: Integrating First-Encounter Semantic Narratives and Physiological Biomarkers for Prospective Stroke Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25272)
*Hemn Khdr,Mohammad Noaeen,Karim Keshavjee,Aziz Guergachi,Zahra Shakeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedGate-Fusion多模态门控架构，融合首次就诊叙事文本的Transformer嵌入与10项常规风险标志物，用于初级保健中的前瞻性卒中风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初级保健中的前瞻性卒中风险分层具有挑战性，因为早期风险信号分散在常规生物标志物和非结构化临床叙事中，单一模态难以充分捕捉。

Method: 采用加拿大初级保健哨点监测网络（CPCSSN）电子病历数据，从808,921条就诊级观测中构建首次就诊队列，保留102,736份叙事非空且数据足以评估五年卒中结局的独特患者记录；对卒中相关术语进行基于词典的脱敏处理以降低目标泄漏，随后将叙事文本经Transformer语义编码，并与十项常规记录的风险标志物通过多模态门控架构进行融合。

Result: 摘要未报告具体性能指标或对比结果，仅描述了数据规模、队列构建流程及去泄漏策略。

Conclusion: 该工作表明通过门控机制融合文本语义与结构化风险标志物，并配合词典脱敏，可为初级保健场景下的前瞻性卒中风险分层提供可行路径。

Abstract: Prospective stroke risk stratification in primary care is challenging because early risk signals are distributed across routine biomarkers and unstructured clinical narratives. We propose MedGate-Fusion, a multi-modal gated architecture that integrates transformer-based embeddings of first-encounter narratives with ten routinely recorded risk markers. We used electronic medical record data from the Canadian Primary Care Sentinel Surveillance Network (CPCSSN). Starting from 808,921 encounter-level observations, we constructed a first-encounter cohort and retained 102,736 unique patient records with non-empty narratives and sufficient data to evaluate a five-year stroke outcome. To reduce explicit target leakage from diagnostic mentions in notes, we applied dictionary-based redaction of stroke-related terms prior to semantic encoding.

</details>


### [199] [When LLM Agents Fail to Read the Room: ReAdapt for Relational Social Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25284)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Yunda Liu,Fei Wang,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出关系推理基准与ReAdapt方法，通过显式结构化社交状态与Adapt步骤，使LLM智能体在社会决策中能依据关系证据修正初始选择，显著提升温暖引荐与反应选择任务的准确率并降低oracle regret。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交智能体的基本决策（是否回应帖子、联系谁）并非纯内容问题，正确答案常取决于人际关系中的隐性因素（关系强度、互惠性、共同连接），而非最显著的内容。标准LLM智能体循环未显式表示新关系证据如何修正当前社交假设，当关系线索与内容线索冲突时，易倾向于表面显然的选择。作者希望形式化并解决这一失败模式。

Method: 作者构建关系推理基准：500个合成社交世界（含好友、关注、互动历史和信息流），生成两个任务共1,000条查询——反应选择与温暖引荐（寻找通往目标人物的最佳桥梁）。构造上，约53%查询中表面显然候选与关系依据的oracle不同，形成必须用关系证据推翻初始选择的overturn子集。提出ReAdapt（Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate），在ReAct循环上增加显式结构化社交状态z=(G,B,R,N,D)，分别表示目标、信念、关系、规范与披露。每次工具观测后执行类型化Adapt步骤，更新该状态并输出策略操作（continue、switch、abandon或clarify），再选择下一动作。

Result: 在Gemini-3-Flash上，每任务分层抽取n=150条查询：温暖引荐准确率从37%提升至51%（+14个百分点），反应选择准确率从69%提升至77%（+8个百分点）；oracle regret分别从0.260降至0.152、从0.095降至0.053。模型、工具与环境保持不变。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式的关系状态自适应有助于LLM智能体将检索到的社交证据转化为修正后的决策，从而在关系线索与内容线索冲突时做出更符合关系依据的选择。

Abstract: A social agent's most basic decisions (should I react to this post? who should I reach out to?) are not purely content problems. The right action often hinges on the latent relationship between people -- tie strength, reciprocity, mutual connections -- rather than on which content is most salient. Standard LLM agent loops do not explicitly represent how new relational evidence should revise the agent's current social hypothesis, leaving them prone to surface-obvious choices when relational and content cues diverge. We formalize this failure mode with a relationship-reasoning benchmark: 500 synthetic social worlds with friendships, follows, reaction histories, and feeds, yielding 1,000 queries over two tasks, reaction selection and warm introduction (finding the best bridge to a target person). By construction, the surface-obvious candidate differs from the relationship-grounded oracle in about 53% of queries, forming an overturn subset where the agent must use relational evidence to revise an initially plausible choice. We propose ReAdapt (Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate), which augments the ReAct loop with an explicit structured social state z = (G, B, R, N, D) capturing goal, belief, relationship, norm, and disclosure. After each tool observation, ReAdapt runs a typed Adapt step that updates this state and emits a policy operation (continue, switch, abandon, or clarify) before choosing the next action. With Gemini-3-Flash on a stratified subset of n = 150 queries per task, ReAdapt improves warm-introduction accuracy from 37% to 51% (+14 points) and reaction-selection accuracy from 69% to 77% (+8 points). Oracle regret drops from 0.260 to 0.152 and from 0.095 to 0.053, respectively. Holding the model, tools, and environments fixed, these results suggest that explicit relational-state adaptation helps LLM agents turn retrieved social evidence into revised decisions.

</details>


### [200] [Attention as a Routing Graph: Live Circuit Extraction from a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25285)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"低成本草图"方法：仅用一次前向传播将注意力视为路由图，提取少量指向答案的路径，并验证其因果重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型电路发现需要大量精细干预，成本高昂。作者希望用更简单的方式获得具有因果信号的电路草图。

Method: 将注意力解释为一次前向传播中的路由图，保留少量指向答案的路由；通过在已知任务（induction、IOI）上消融提取边并与同规模随机边对比，使用配对间隙检验与bootstrap置信区间评估；在GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium、Pythia-410M上每格n=100提示进行实验。

Result: 消融提取边对模型的损害显著大于消融同规模随机边，表明提取路由具有真实因果作用；提取步骤仅需一次前向，而逐头补丁扫描成本约高两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法并非完整电路图谱，而是一种在已知任务上携带真实因果信号的廉价草图，并明确了失效模式；代码与评估材料已开源。

Abstract: Finding circuits in language models usually means running many careful interventions. We try something simpler: treat attention as a routing map from one forward pass, keep a small set of routes that point toward the answer, and ask whether those routes actually matter.
  They often do. On induction and IOI (tasks where the "right" circuit is already known), ablating our extracted edges hurts the model much more than ablating a random set of the same size. We evaluate n=100 prompts per cell on GPT-2 Small, GPT-2 Medium, and Pythia-410M, with paired gap tests and bootstrap confidence intervals. The extract step costs one forward; a head-by-head patch sweep costs about two orders of magnitude more.
  We are not claiming a complete circuit atlas. We are claiming a cheap sketch that carries real causal signal on known tasks, with clear failure modes when it does not. Code and evaluation artifacts are at https://github.com/Aquinf03/live-circuit-routing.

</details>


### [201] [Learned Enterprise Data Comprehension: Compression and Routing for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25286)
*Ethan Torres,Eric Mills*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"潜在等价学习"框架，将任务相关的持久身份与数据集相对的具体实现分离，通过支持/反对证据学习的高斯原型和查询-原型系统实现身份因子化、查询条件化的证据路由，在Data Agent Benchmark上以94.67% Pass@1大幅超越参考基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级结构化数据智能体需在跨模式、关系、策略与业务角色的复杂环境中推理，证据高度分散。现有系统依赖可复用的markdown式记忆或技能文件保存已发现信息，虽减少重复探索，却掩盖了合理的分工：智能体擅长语义推理，而学习系统更适合预测与组织重复出现的结构。

Method: 提出潜在等价学习：将持久、任务相关的身份与其数据集相对的具体实现分离。支持性与反对性证据共同塑造"支持实现的高斯原型"，学习这些身份在特定数据环境中的表达方式，并以软隶属度profile保留硬分配所丢失的区分信息。另设学习的查询-原型系统表示重复出现的证据需求，通过学习的兼容性函数映射到同一持久身份结构中。该身份因子化、查询条件化的路由机制在下游推理前即物化出相关的数据集特定证据。

Result: 在Data Agent Benchmark（覆盖12个异构数据集、54条查询）上，完整实现于五次完整试验中取得94.67%的数据集宏观分层Pass@1，原始查询尝试成功258/270；相比之下，基准自带的Claude Opus 4.6参考智能体仅为55.51%。提交时在40个排行榜条目中位列第一。

Conclusion: 通过潜在等价学习将持久身份与数据集相对实现解耦，并以查询条件化路由预先组织证据状态，智能体无需在每次查询时重建跨模式结构，从而在异构企业结构化数据任务上显著超越强基线并取得领先排名。

Abstract: Structured-data agents in enterprise settings must reason over complex data environments whose relevant evidence is distributed across schemas, relationships, policies, and recurring business roles. Modern agentic systems often address this burden through reusable markdown-style memory or skill files that preserve previously discovered information for later queries, reducing the need to rediscover the same structure repeatedly. This is useful, but it obscures a natural division of labor: agents are well suited to semantic reasoning, while learned systems are well suited to predicting and organizing recurring structure. We introduce latent equivalence learning to bridge this gap. The framework separates persistent task-relevant identities from their dataset-relative realizations. In our realization, supporting and opposing evidence shape support-realized Gaussian prototypes that learn how those identities are expressed in a particular data environment, while soft-membership profiles retain distinctions lost under a hard assignment. A separate learned query-prototype system represents recurring evidential requirements and maps them through a learned compatibility function into the same persistent identity structure. This identity-factorized, query-conditioned routing materializes the relevant dataset-specific evidence for downstream reasoning, allowing the agent to operate over an already organized evidential state rather than reconstructing cross-schema structure at every query. On the Data Agent Benchmark, spanning 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, our full implementation achieves 94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 over five complete trials and 258/270 successful raw query attempts, compared with 55.51% for the benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent, ranking first among 40 leaderboard entries at submission.

</details>


### [202] [Making Agents More Consistent: Skills Should Form Habits for Repeat Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25299)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 智能体在重复任务上表现不一致且有大量浪费，作者提出"技能习惯形成"方法，通过从执行历史中挖掘确定性技能变体、以四道递增成本的门控筛选，使系统在文本到SQL任务上实现可复现的稳定输出并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 买家、审计者和监管者都要求一致性，但智能体并不具备这一特性：42项任务各运行3次，依模型不同有38%~74%的结果不一致。同时智能体极其浪费，95.3%~97.2%的生成内容用于重新推导系统已知的计划。当智能体被纳入受监管或可审计的场景时，缺乏可复现性成为根本障碍。

Method: 提出"技能习惯形成"：智能体从自身执行历史中挖掘候选技能——它是与现有方案竞争的确定性变体，而非直接替代。候选技能声明其覆盖的输入空间区域，常见情形以脚本执行，其余情形回落至推理。四道门槛按成本递增筛选候选，核心门槛用保留的参考轨迹检验候选执行轨迹，容差由该参考自身逐次运行的波动性度量得到。此外，将路由与参数抽取分离。

Result: 在文本到SQL任务上，四个推理分支中有三个在42个重复问题中仅11~13个复现了自身输出，第四个为26个；而习惯形成变体在重复的456次派遣中全部复现，且对它所替代的各分支均非劣（p<0.0001），token用量减少14%~56%，在7~53次复用后转为净收益。门控在2.6%的自然改写和26%的边界附近输入上错误放行，其中13例失败中有11例在任何阈值下都无法被轨迹一致性门发现。将路由与参数抽取分离后，端到端准确率从0.888提升至0.952，成本仅为原来的43%。

Conclusion: 确定性错误会精确重复：坏习惯与好习惯同样可靠，这正是使系统可审计所付出的代价，但可审计性本身即是价值所在。技能习惯形成以确定性变体竞争取代一次性生成，在保证一致性的同时降低token消耗，且分离路由与参数抽取可显著提升准确率并降低成本。

Abstract: On repeated work, agents are inconsistent. We ran 42 tasks three times each and found that, depending on the model, 38% to 74% returned answers that did not agree. Consistency is what a buyer, an auditor, or a regulator requires, and agents do not have it. They are wasteful too: 95.3% to 97.2% of what an agent generates goes to re-deriving a plan the system already knows.
  We propose skill habit formation. An agent mines its own execution history for candidate skills, deterministic variants that compete against the incumbent rather than replacing it. A candidate declares the region of input space it claims, so the common case runs as a script and the rest falls through to reasoning. Four gates of ascending cost admit candidates; the central one tests a candidate's execution trace against a retained reference, within a tolerance measured from that reference's own run-to-run variability.
  On text-to-SQL, three of four reasoning arms reproduced their own output on 11 to 13 of 42 repeated questions and the fourth on 26 of 42, while a habit-formed variant reproduced on all 456 dispatches we repeated and was non-inferior to every arm it replaced (p<0.0001). It also used 14% to 56% fewer tokens, turning net positive after 7 to 53 reuses.
  We measured what this costs in accuracy. The guard admitted work it should have deferred on 2.6% of natural paraphrases and 26% of inputs near its boundary, and 11 of 13 such failures were invisible to the trace-conformance gate at any threshold. Deterministic errors repeat exactly: a bad habit is as reliable as a good one, and that is the price of the property that makes the system auditable. Separating routing from parameter extraction raised end-to-end accuracy from 0.888 to 0.952 at 43% of the cost.

</details>


### [203] [Potential for Enhanced Learning in Machine Learning Classes by Using Wiki LLM Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25303)
*Brian Wright*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在同课程语料上对比向量RAG与LLM编译wiki：两者单点事实题相当，但跨单元关联题wiki显著更优且更可溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型作为课程助教的价值依赖经过审核的教学材料，而这些材料常在学期中途修订；作者前期构建的多模态RAG系统虽改善了语境接地，但固定检索策略表现不佳，因此需要验证：语料在入库阶段的结构化方式是否比查询阶段的检索量更关键。

Method: 在同一课堂语料（《机器学习基础》）上进行受控对照实验：表征A为向量RAG（复现前期最优配置），表征B为LLM编译wiki（Karpathy框架），在入库时将语料综合为带显式交叉引用与原文引证的关联概念页。评估59个问题，覆盖单点事实回忆、跨单元概念关联、综合解释以及大纲修订后的时效性，由LLM评审依据人工编写的评分量规打分。

Result: 单点事实题两者接近（9.33 vs. 9.96/10）；跨单元关联题差异显著：编译wiki准确且接地（9.93，100%有引证来源），向量检索更低且接地率明显不足（8.14，64%）。wiki的引证可追溯至最初讲授该内容的讲座，形成一层动态检索支持。

Conclusion: 入库时的语料结构化方式可能比查询时的检索量更重要；用于机器学习课程教学的AI应优先考虑基于wiki的知识结构，以支撑最佳实践的基础要素，但仍需进一步测试。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as course-specific tutors, but their usefulness depends on grounding in vetted instructional materials that are often revised mid-semester. Our prior work built a multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system over an authentic machine learning course corpus (Foundations of Machine Learning) and found that retrieval improved contextual grounding, but that fixed retrieval strategies were suboptimal. That motivates a different question: whether how a corpus is structured at ingest time matters more than how much is retrieved at query time. We present a controlled head-to-head comparison of two knowledge representations over an identical classroom corpus: (A) vector RAG, replicating the best-performing configuration from our prior study, and (B) an LLM-compiled wiki (Karpathy framework), in which the corpus is synthesized at ingest into linked concept pages with explicit cross-references and citations back to source materials. We evaluate 59 questions spanning single-fact recall, cross-unit concept linking, synthesis and explanation, and currency after a syllabus revision, scored by an LLM judge against a human-authored rubric. Both representations answered single-fact questions about equally well (9.33 vs. 9.96 of 10), but diverged sharply on questions requiring links across course units. The compiled wiki remained accurate and grounded (9.93; 100% grounded in cited sources), while retrieval scored lower and was markedly less grounded (8.14; 64%). The wiki's citations let students and instructors trace any claim back to the lecture that introduced it, adding a layer of dynamic retrieval that machine learning courses require. While further testing is needed, instructors using AI to support learning in ML courses should consider wiki-based structure for its potential to support foundational elements of best practice.

</details>


### [204] [Clarification Is Not Correction: LLMs Fail to Let Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25337)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Fei Wang,Robert Douglas,Rajeshkumar Golani,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对话失败常被归因为"记忆失效"，但本文指出更根本的问题是模型"过早承诺"：模糊的早期话轮塌缩为单一隐含解释，后续澄清被当作额外上下文而非纠错信号，导致顺序效应。作者提出"早期后验塌缩"概念，并在写作、规划、编码任务中验证，发现编码任务尤其脆弱，标准提示与记忆策略均无法可靠缓解，进而提出"保留不确定性的状态管理"方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将对话失败归因于记忆问题（上下文过长、摘要失真、约束遗忘），但作者认为这忽略了更深层的问题：模型并非忘记，而是在歧义尚未解决时就过早锁定了单一解释。后续澄清只能在该"陈旧任务状态"上做微调，而无法使其失效，从而产生顺序效应。因此需要重新定位交互式LLM的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 作者提出"早期后验塌缩（early posterior collapse）"概念，即未解决的用户意图在歧义解除前塌缩为已承诺的任务状态。采用写作、规划、编码三类受控对话任务，使用Gemini-2.5-Pro与Gemini-2.5-Flash进行数千次试验，比较相同信息在不同顺序下呈现的结果，即便最终对话包含等价的任务相关信息。同时测试标准提示策略与记忆策略（如摘要、思维链）的效果。

Result: 数千次试验显示，相同信息以不同顺序呈现会产生不同结果，存在明显顺序效应，说明后续澄清被当作额外上下文而非纠错信号，只能细化陈旧任务状态而无法将其推翻。编码任务尤其易受影响，说明早期假设会嵌入接口、控制流等结构化产物中。标准提示与记忆策略无法可靠改善：摘要可能加剧歧义塌缩，思维链虽能减少推理轨迹中的显式错误承诺，却未提升最终任务成功率。

Conclusion: 若助手无法放弃早期解释，鲁棒性就不能仅依赖事后纠正，而必须防止模糊的早期话轮固化为单一任务状态。作者主张转向"保留不确定性的状态管理"：在歧义存在时保持试探性假设，高影响歧义持续时先询问再执行，当后续证据推翻早期解读时从修正后的状态重建。其目标是引导交互式LLM研究从"保留更多上下文"转向"保留不确定性"。

Abstract: Dialogue failures in language models are usually framed as memory failures: context too long, summaries lossy, a constraint forgotten. We argue this misses a deeper problem: in many conversations the model does not forget, it commits too early. An ambiguous early turn collapses into a single hidden interpretation, and later clarification is filtered through that commitment. We call this early posterior collapse: unresolved user intent collapsing into a committed task state before ambiguity is resolved. We study it with controlled dialogue tasks in writing, planning, and coding using Gemini-2.5-Pro and Gemini-2.5-Flash. Across thousands of trials, the same information in different orders yields different outcomes, even when the final dialogue contains equivalent task-relevant information. This order effect suggests later clarification is treated as extra context rather than a corrective signal: it refines a stale task state without invalidating it. Coding tasks are especially vulnerable, suggesting early assumptions get embedded in structured artifacts such as interfaces and control flow. Standard prompting and memory strategies do not reliably help: summaries can collapse ambiguity, and chain-of-thought can reduce explicit wrong commitment in reasoning traces without improving final task success. These findings motivate uncertainty-preserving state management. If assistants cannot let go of early interpretations, robustness cannot rely on post hoc correction alone; it must keep ambiguous early turns from hardening into one task state. Assistants should hold tentative hypotheses while ambiguity remains, ask before executing when high-impact ambiguity persists, and rebuild from a revised state when later evidence invalidates an earlier reading. Rather than one prompting fix, we aim to redirect research for interactive LLMs from retaining more context toward preserving uncertainty.

</details>


### [205] [From Decorative to Load-Bearing: Task Difficulty Shapes the Causal Role of Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25366)
*Renee Jia,Di Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"续写式因果测试"(continuation-based causal testing),通过扰动单个推理步骤、截断思维链并强制模型从被污染前缀续写,测量CoT对最终答案的"承重性"(load-bearingness)。结果显示CoT承重性随任务相对难度系统性变化:简单任务上模型静默绕过自身推理,困难任务上模型跟随被污染的步骤并传播错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控只有在"写出的推理对答案具有因果约束力"时才真正有意义,但既往研究多聚焦机制层面的faithfulness,缺乏对"CoT是否真正承重"这一行为学问题的系统度量。作者希望区分"行为承重性"与"机制忠实性",并检验其随任务难度、扰动类型和训练方式的变化规律,以评估CoT在AI安全监控中的可用性。

Method: 提出continuation-based causal testing(基于续写的消融-补丁干预):扰动单个推理步骤→截断思维链→强制模型从被污染的前缀继续生成,从而度量该CoT对最终答案的承重程度。在Gemma-2-9B-IT、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B三个模型,GSM8K、MMLU、BIG-Bench Hard三个基准上评估。采用2x2匹配分析、对28,584条续写的方差分解,并辅以四变体judge敏感性分析与双标注者盲评(n=500);另用隐状态线性探针与加性激活引导(additive activation steering)考察行为模式的可读性与可控性。

Result: CoT承重性与模型相对任务难度挂钩:简单任务上模型静默绕过推理,困难任务上则跟随被污染步骤并传播错误。2x2分析表明任务难度主导扰动类型:错误传播从GSM8K到BBH多步算术上升16倍;方差分解显示任务难度解释98.8%的可解释偏差,扰动类型仅占0.8%。推理专用RL抑制错误传播并压缩该梯度。四变体judge分析及盲评(n=500)显示"错误传播 vs 非传播"标签对judge提示不变,标注者完全一致(Cohen's kappa=1.00)。隐状态线性探针可区分静默绕过、自我纠正与错误传播三类模式,但加性激活引导的因果控制有限,最多只能翻转约25%的错误传播案例。

Conclusion: CoT承重性呈现结构性梯度:思维链易读之处信号微弱,而真正关键之处错误在监控者介入前已传播,这对基于CoT的监督与AI安全监控构成结构性难题。行为模式虽可被线性探针读取,却无法被现有激活引导手段可靠控制——"可读但不可靠可控"。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is only meaningful if written reasoning causally constrains the answer. We introduce continuation-based causal testing, an ablation-patch intervention that perturbs one reasoning step, truncates the chain, and forces the model to continue from the corrupted prefix.
